Conclusion: 所提出的方法在性能和效率方面都提供了优势。

Abstract: In this work, we address diacritic restoration for Arabic speech transcripts. Most speech data are undiacritized, limiting the ability of modeling fine-grained phonological distinctions. The speech modality has recently been explored as a way to complement text-based diacritic restoration efforts. We propose an efficient non-autoregressive approach for speech-to-text diacritization based on Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC). Our method incorporates hard constraints during decoding by constructing a character-level diacritization lattice from an undiacritized transcript and restricting hypotheses to valid diacritized realizations. We evaluate on Classical Arabic and Modern Standard Arabic test sets (namely, ArVoice and ClArTTS) against a more computationally-complex multi-modal diacritic restoration baseline, and show statistically significant reductions in diacritic error rates in both, demonstrating that the proposed approach offers both performance and efficiency gains.

</details>


### [28] [Verifiable Self-Evolution for Open-Ended Dialogue Skills via Future-Feedback Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18973)
*ChaoJin Zhao,Xuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为future-feedback skill evolution的方法，用于在开放对话中实现基于文本技能的自我进化。该方法通过预测已观察到的回答是否会导致后续用户的正向或负向信号，将移动的对话反馈转化为固定的离线学习目标，从而支持验证门控的文本优化。在销售助手数据集上，该方法实现了超过75%的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放对话中，传统的自我进化方法需要稳定的验证信号，但改变助手回答会改变用户后续反应，导致日志记录的反应无法直接评估反事实回答。因此，需要一种方法将动态的对话反馈转化为固定的离线学习目标，以实现可复现的技能进化。

Method: future-feedback skill evolution方法首先将自我进化从规定当前答案转向预测已观察到的答案是否会导致正向或负向的后续用户信号。该预测任务可在固定的日志元组上进行验证，从而支持验证门控的文本优化。进化后的反馈技能可捕获可解释的响应质量标准，并可作为答案技能的诊断和优化目标。

Result: 在专有的隐私保护销售助手数据集上，通过仔细的质量过滤和平衡的已解决/未解决分割，预测准确率超过75%。该方法成功将移动的对话反馈转化为固定的离线学习目标，实现了无需将每个候选技能置于实时流量中的可复现技能进化。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是一种将对话反馈转化为固定离线学习目标的公式化方法，使验证门控的文本优化成为可能。该方法定位为离线优化阶段，而非最终人类或在线评估的替代品。文章还讨论了观察性验证与反事实有效性之间的边界。

Abstract: Textual skills provide a lightweight way to improve frozen language-model agents, but their self-evolution normally requires a stable validation signal. Such signals are natural in mathematics or code, where an answer can be checked after it changes, yet are problematic in open-ended dialogue: changing the assistant response also changes the user's next reaction, so a logged reaction cannot directly evaluate a counterfactual response. We propose future-feedback skill evolution, which first redirects self-evolution from prescribing the current answer to predicting whether the observed answer will lead to a positive or negative subsequent user signal. This prediction task is verifiable on fixed logged tuples and therefore supports validation-gated textual optimization. The evolved feedback skill captures interpretable criteria for response quality and can subsequently serve as a diagnostic and optimization target for answer skills. On a proprietary, privacy-preserving sales-assistant dataset, careful quality filtering and a balanced resolved/unresolved split yield more than 75% prediction accuracy. Beyond this result, the central contribution is a formulation that converts otherwise moving conversational feedback into a fixed offline learning target, enabling reproducible skill evolution without placing every candidate skill in live traffic. We discuss the boundary between observational verification and counterfactual validity, and position the method as an offline optimization stage rather than a replacement for final human or online evaluation.

</details>


### [29] [AutoJourn: Multi-Perspective Summarisation, Bias Detection and Bias Neutralisation for LLM-Generated News in Automated Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18983)
*Himel Ghosh,Ahmed Mosharafa,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoJourn是一个用于多视角新闻生成和偏见感知评估的演示系统，基于大语言模型（LLMs）构建。该系统解决了负责任自动化新闻的三个核心挑战：从非结构化社交媒体讨论中提取多样化观点、生成保留视角多样性的摘要、以及检测或减轻AI生成新闻中的偏见。管道集成了高级提示工程和可选的检索增强以产生语义多样化的视角集，一个多视角摘要模块将冲突观点合并为平衡摘要，以及一个偏见分析套件支持句子级偏见检测和类型分类，并自动中立化。用户可以在界面中检查视角聚类、比较立场特定摘要、生成新闻文章，并直接应用偏见感知重写。我们通过内在指标评估每个组件——语义多样性、摘要质量、偏见减少——并显示相对于强基线的改进，同时保持内容保真度。一个公开可访问的实时演示伴随论文以促进可重复性和对社会负责的自动化新闻的进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在新闻生成中的应用，确保自动化新闻的负责任性变得至关重要。现有工作主要关注生成质量，但缺乏对多视角多样性和偏见检测与缓解的系统性处理。AutoJourn旨在填补这一空白，通过构建一个端到端系统，从社交媒体中提取多样化观点，生成平衡的新闻摘要，并提供偏见分析工具，以促进更公平、更透明的自动化新闻生产。

Method: AutoJourn系统包含三个核心模块：1) 视角提取模块：使用高级提示工程和可选的检索增强，从社交媒体讨论中提取语义多样化的观点集合；2) 多视角摘要模块：将提取的冲突观点合并为平衡的摘要，保留不同立场；3) 偏见分析套件：支持句子级偏见检测、偏见类型分类，并自动进行中立化重写。系统提供交互式界面，允许用户检查视角聚类、比较立场特定摘要、生成新闻文章并应用偏见感知重写。

Result: 通过内在指标评估，AutoJourn在语义多样性、摘要质量和偏见减少方面均优于强基线方法，同时保持内容保真度。具体而言，视角提取模块能够产生更丰富的观点集合；多视角摘要模块在保持信息完整性的同时实现了更好的平衡；偏见分析套件有效检测并减轻了生成新闻中的偏见。公开演示系统已上线，支持可重复性研究。

Conclusion: AutoJourn为负责任自动化新闻提供了一个全面的解决方案，通过整合多视角提取、平衡摘要生成和偏见分析，显著提升了AI生成新闻的公平性和透明度。该系统不仅展示了技术可行性，也为未来研究提供了可扩展的框架和公开资源，推动社会负责的自动化新闻发展。

Abstract: We present AutoJourn, a demonstration system for multi-perspective news generation and bias-aware evaluation using large language models (LLMs). The system tackles three core challenges in responsible automated journalism: extracting diverse perspectives from unstructured social media discussions, generating summaries that preserve viewpoint diversity, and detecting or mitigating bias in AI-generated news. The pipeline integrates advanced prompt engineering with optional retrieval augmentation to produce semantically diverse perspective sets, a multi-perspective summarisation module that merges conflicting viewpoints into balanced summaries, and a bias analysis suite supporting sentence-level bias detection and type classification in the generated news article, and automatic neutralisation. Users can inspect perspective clusters, compare stance-specific summaries, generate news articles, and apply bias-aware rewrites directly in the interface. We evaluate each component with intrinsic metrics -- semantic diversity, summary quality, and bias reduction and show improvements over strong baselines while maintaining content fidelity. A live, publicly accessible demo accompanies the paper to facilitate reproducibility and further research on socially responsible automated journalism.

</details>


### [30] [Disentangling Curriculum Learning in NLP: Towards a Unifying Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18984)
*Vanessa Toborek,Florian Seiffarth,Sebastian Müller,Tamás Horváth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出细粒度分类法，将课程学习（CL）中的难度评估与训练调度分离，揭示NLP领域CL策略的系统不可比性问题，并提供形式化框架以支持设计、分析与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CL在NLP中研究超过十年，但缺乏针对特定问题选择难度函数或调度的原则性指导，导致不同工作间难以比较和积累证据。

Method: 提出细粒度分类法：1）难度评估区分归因来源与任务依赖性，将难度视为视角性概念；2）调度以预期训练贡献形式化，引入保留机制与单调性性质。通过分析NLP中CL工作，诊断系统不可比性问题。

Result: 分类法揭示先前工作混淆不同难度概念与调度目标，虽同称CL但追求不同目标，阻碍比较与证据积累。分类法支持CL策略设计、分析与比较，并推动 disentangle 改进来源的评估实践。

Conclusion: 通过分离难度评估与调度，提供诊断与改进CL研究的框架，强调需明确概念以促进领域进展。

Abstract: Despite more than a decade of curriculum learning (CL) research in NLP, the field lacks a principled account of which difficulty function or scheduler to use for a given problem. To understand what has hindered progress towards this account, we propose a fine-grained taxonomy separating difficulty evaluation from training scheduling to enable systematic analysis of CL strategies. For difficulty evaluation, we distinguish attribution source and task dependence, revealing difficulty as a perspectival concept encoding different assumptions about what makes an instance hard to learn. For scheduling, we provide the first formalisation of CL schedulers in terms of expected training contribution, enabling comparison across implementations by introducing retention regimes and monotonicity properties. Applied in a dedicated analysis of CL works in NLP, our taxonomy reveals a systematic incomparability problem: prior works conflate distinct notions of difficulty and scheduling, often pursuing different objectives under the same CL label -- hindering comparison and the accumulation of a coherent evidence base. Beyond diagnosis, the taxonomy supports the design, analysis, and comparison of CL strategies, and motivates evaluation practices that disentangle the sources of observed improvement.

</details>


### [31] [MedDDC-Eval: Diagnosis-Decoupled Evaluation of Multi-Turn Medical Consultation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18999)
*Guofeng Zhang,Yizeng Quan,Huaiyi Fang,Jianwei Lv,Jinyao Liu,Xunxu Duan,Lening An,Yu Ouyang,Junfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedDDC-Eval，一个诊断解耦的评估框架，用于评估多轮医疗咨询代理在信息收集和诊断决策方面的表现。通过固定历史到诊断的映射，该框架能够独立评估策略所收集的病史质量。实验表明，诊断读者的变化会显著影响诊断F1分数，且使用GRPO训练的策略在诊断结果和轨迹反馈上均有提升。该框架支持可控归因、可解释的病史测量和评估引导的证据采集策略开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮医疗咨询代理的耦合评估方法将策略生成的病史质量与终端诊断生成能力混在一起，导致无法独立评估信息采集策略的有效性。强诊断生成能力可能掩盖病史收集不足，而弱诊断生成能力可能低估丰富病史的价值。因此需要一种解耦的评估方法，以准确衡量策略在证据采集方面的表现。

Method: 提出MedDDC-Eval诊断解耦测试平台：1) 固定历史到诊断的映射，使用共享冻结的阅读器（frozen reader）处理所有策略生成的病史，从而隔离病史质量对诊断结果的影响；2) 设计两个评估维度：grounded interface（接地界面）和auditable diagnosis-trajectory-efficiency (D/T/E) harness（可审计诊断轨迹效率 harness），分别衡量诊断有用性、信息获取和效率；3) 采用方向性语义覆盖（directional semantic coverage）结合确定性一对一分配（deterministic one-to-one assignment）计算开放域项目的精确率-召回率，每个预测或参考最多匹配一次；4) 使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 基于交互式多轮回滚，使用诊断结果和轨迹反馈对Qwen3-32B进行后训练。

Result: 1) 固定病史后，仅改变诊断阅读器导致诊断F1分数变化2.2-19.0点，并在Record和Dialogue划分上分别逆转18%和36%的策略成对排序；2) 在100例Record划分和70例Dialogue划分上，经GRPO训练的策略相比初始化分别提升9.7和4.6总分点；3) 移除任一主要信号（诊断结果或轨迹反馈）都会降低保留集上的联合性能。

Conclusion: MedDDC-Eval框架通过解耦病史收集与诊断生成，实现了对医疗咨询代理证据采集策略的独立、可解释评估。实验证明该框架支持可控归因，并能有效指导基于评估反馈的策略优化。诊断读者的选择对评估结果有显著影响，而结合诊断结果与轨迹反馈的GRPO训练能提升策略性能。该框架为多轮医疗咨询代理的开发提供了可靠的评估与优化工具。

Abstract: Multi-turn medical consultation agents must decide what to ask, adapt to patient responses, and determine when the collected evidence is sufficient. However, coupled evaluation conflates the quality of the policy-elicited history with policy-specific terminal diagnosis generation: strong generation can compensate for a thin history, while weaker generation can obscure a rich one. We introduce MedDDC-Eval, a diagnosis-decoupled testbed that treats elicited history as the comparison object and holds the history-to-diagnosis mapping constant through a shared frozen reader. Across two held-out sources, a grounded interface and an auditable diagnosis-trajectory-efficiency (D/T/E) harness measure diagnostic usefulness, information acquisition, and efficiency. Directional semantic coverage followed by deterministic one-to-one assignment yields coherent precision-recall counts for open-ended items, with at most one credited match per prediction or reference. Holding histories fixed, changing only the diagnostic reader shifts diagnosis F1 by 2.2-19.0 points and reverses 18% and 36% of pairwise policy orderings on the Record and Dialogue splits. We further apply standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) over interactive multi-turn rollouts to post-train Qwen3-32B using diagnosis-result and trajectory feedback. On the 100-case Record and 70-case Dialogue splits, the trained policy improves over its initialization by 9.7 and 4.6 total-score points; removing either primary signal lowers held-out joint performance. These results show that MedDDC-Eval supports controlled attribution, interpretable elicited-history measurement, and evaluation-guided evidence-acquisition policy development.

</details>


### [32] [Computational Humor with Multimodal LLMs: Methods, Datasets, Evaluation, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19011)
*Tuo Liang,Zhe Hu,Disheng Liu,Jing Li,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态幽默理解与生成的研究进展，提出基于能力的层次框架，涵盖识别、解释推理和生成三个层面，并指出当前面临的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态幽默（如表情包、漫画、卡通）的理解对AI系统而言极具挑战性，因为其含义依赖于非字面机制、共享文化知识和交际意图，而非简单的场景描述。本文旨在系统梳理该领域的研究现状，并与已有幽默、讽刺及多模态大模型综述区分开来，构建一个能力导向的分类体系。

Method: 采用能力中心层次结构（recognition, interpretation & reasoning, generation）组织文献，综合评述了基准设计、评估协议和建模范式，追踪了从任务特定融合模型到基于大规模对齐、证据推理和可控生成的演进路径。

Result: 研究发现领域正从任务特定模型转向大模型方法，但仍存在评估易受捷径影响、文化和叙事覆盖有限、证据 grounding 薄弱以及安全与所有权问题未解决等主要障碍。

Conclusion: 未来需在评估稳健性、文化多样性、证据可追溯性及伦理规范等方面取得突破，以推动多模态幽默AI的健康发展。

Abstract: Multimodal humor in memes, cartoons, and comics remains difficult for AI systems because intended meaning depends on non-literal mechanisms, shared cultural knowledge, and communicative intent rather than literal scene description. This survey focuses on visual humor understanding in single-image and multi-panel artifacts, while treating humor generation as an emerging downstream frontier. We position the literature against prior humor, sarcasm, and general MLLM surveys and organize it using a capability-centric hierarchy spanning recognition, interpretation and reasoning, and generation. Under this lens, we synthesize benchmark design, evaluation protocols, and modeling paradigms, tracing the field's shift from task-specific fusion models to large-model approaches based on multimodal alignment, evidence-grounded reasoning, and controlled generation. We conclude by highlighting the main barriers to progress: shortcut-prone evaluation, limited cultural and narrative coverage, weak evidence grounding, and unresolved safety and ownership concerns.

</details>


### [33] [Content is What Remains: Invariant Speech Tokenization from Parallel Utterances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19033)
*Laurin Wagner,Bernhard Thallinger,Miroslav Stankovic,Mario Zusag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PINT是一种通过并行对齐和增强来微调自监督学习编码器的方法，旨在从离散语音标记中消除非语言信息（如说话人身份、韵律和信道条件），从而显著降低标记熵并提高语义一致性。实验表明，PINT在说话人探针准确率、ABX错误率和语言模型困惑度上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自监督学习（如HuBERT）的离散语音标记器仍保留非语言信息，导致标记熵增加。核心观点是：当足够多的说话人在不同条件下说出相同词汇时，语言内容是唯一共享因素。因此，需要一种方法来提取这种共享的残差，实现更纯净的语义标记。

Method: PINT通过并行对齐损失（alignment losses）和增强（augmentations）对SSL编码器进行微调。具体而言，利用同一内容由不同说话人在不同条件下说出的并行语料，强制模型将相同词汇映射到一致的标记序列，从而消除与说话人、信道等相关的变异。

Result: 1) 说话人探针准确率相对降低98.7%（从93.1%降至1.2%）；2) ABX错误率降低42%；3) 语言模型困惑度降低27-30%。这些结果证明PINT有效减少了非语言信息，同时保留了帧级时间对齐，可作为音频编解码器的语义目标。

Conclusion: PINT通过利用并行语料和增强，成功从SSL特征中蒸馏出语言内容，实现了高度一致的离散标记。实验验证了该方法在降低非语言变异、提高语义纯度方面的有效性，为高效语音表示学习提供了关键途径。

Abstract: Discrete speech tokenizers aim to disentangle semantic from acoustic information, yet targets from self-supervised learning (SSL) models like HuBERT retain non-linguistic variation: speaker identity, prosody, and channel conditions leak into the tokens, inflating entropy. Our key insight is that when enough speakers utter the same words under varying conditions, linguistic content is the only shared factor. We propose PINT (Parallel INvariant Tokenization), which fine-tunes an SSL encoder with alignment losses across parallel utterances and augmentations to distill this shared residual. PINT collapses identical words onto consistent token sequences, drastically reducing conditional entropy. Unlike ASR text, PINT tokens preserve frame-level temporal grounding and serve as drop-in semantic targets for audio codecs. Experiments show a 98.7% relative reduction in speaker probe accuracy (93.1% to 1.2%), a 42% lower ABX error rate, and 27-30% lower LM perplexity versus baselines, confirming that the right invariance is key to efficient learning.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmarking Human and Automatic Speech Recognition of Diverse Speech: Initial Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19049)
*Ilse Huisman,Rares Popa,Yuanyuan Zhang,Odette Scharenborg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了最先进的自动语音识别（ASR）系统与荷兰母语者在识别多样化语音（荷兰儿童、老年人语音及弗拉芒口音）上的表现。结果显示，Google Telephony表现最佳，且ASR系统整体表现与人类听者相当，在某些情况下甚至更优。差异与说话者年龄、地区口音及语句长度相关。研究指出ASR系统在应对与年龄和地区口音相关的声学变异性方面仍需改进，并强调测试集选择对比较人类与ASR性能结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为人类是最佳的语音识别者，代表了ASR系统的性能上限。本研究旨在通过实际比较，评估当前最先进的ASR系统与人类在识别多样化语音（包括儿童、老年人及弗拉芒口音）时的表现，以验证这一假设并识别ASR系统的局限性。

Method: 研究选取了荷兰儿童、老年人及弗拉芒口音语音作为测试数据。将多个最先进的ASR系统（包括Google Telephony等）与荷兰母语听者进行对比，通过识别准确率等指标评估两者在多样化语音上的表现。同时，分析了说话者年龄、地区口音及语句长度对性能差异的影响，并比较了不同测试集（Jasmin-CGN子集与完整集）对结论的影响。

Result: 1. Google Telephony在测试的ASR系统中表现最佳。2. 整体上，ASR系统的性能与人类听者相当，在某些情况下（如特定口音或年龄组）甚至优于人类。3. 性能差异与说话者年龄、地区口音及语句长度相关。4. 测试集的选择（如Jasmin-CGN子集与完整集）会影响人类与ASR性能比较的结论。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的ASR系统在识别多样化语音时已能达到与人类听者相当的水平，但在应对与年龄相关的声学变异性（如儿童、老年人语音）和地区口音（如弗拉芒）时仍需提升鲁棒性。此外，基准测试中测试集的选择对比较结果的解释至关重要，未来研究应使用更具代表性的测试集。

Abstract: Humans are often considered to be the best listeners and seen as the upper-bound performance of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. We present a preliminary comparison of the performances of state-of-the-art ASR systems and Dutch native listeners on the recognition of "diverse" speech, specifically Dutch child and older adults' speech and Flemish. Google Telephony outperformed the other ASR systems. Importantly, the ASR systems showed similar performance to the listeners, and in specific cases even outperformed them. Slight performance differences between the listeners and ASR systems were found related to speaker's age and regional accents and utterance length. Future research should focus on making ASR systems more robust to acoustic variability related to aging and regional accents. A comparison of ASR recognition performances on the test stimuli and the full Jasmin-CGN test sets showed the influence of the specific test sets on the conclusions regarding benchmarking human and ASR performance.

</details>


### [35] [DAIS: Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19088)
*Yu Wang,Ming Fan,Xicheng Zhang,Zhiyong Li,Zhihu Wang,Caiyue Xu,Dahai Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DAIS框架，通过将教师推理转化为阶段级QA记录，引入依赖感知的中间监督，在多个基准上显著提升最终答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CoT监督仅优化单一推理序列，缺乏对局部结论如何支撑后续决策的监督，导致中间推理与最终答案的关联性不足。

Method: DAIS将过滤后的教师推理转化为阶段级QA记录：每个中间记录基于先前状态预测局部答案，最终答案记录保持原始任务格式；推理时仅使用原始输入和可选上下文。

Result: 在GDPR、AIACT、MedQA和FOLIO数据集上，使用多个Qwen模型，DAIS在最终答案准确率上优于仅答案、平坦CoT和独立QA基线；在政策合规基准上最大提升5.6%，平均提升4.2%。

Conclusion: 依赖条件化的中间QA作为轻量级辅助监督信号，能有效提升标准最终答案推理性能，且其收益超过单纯延长目标或增加中间文本带来的效果。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision exposes intermediate rationales, but flat rationale targets usually optimize a single reasoning sequence and provide limited supervision on how local conclusions should support later decisions. We introduce Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision (DAIS), a training-time framework that converts filtered teacher rationales into stage-level QA records. Each intermediate record predicts a local answer conditioned on the previous states needed for that decision, while the final-answer record keeps the original task format; evaluation therefore uses only the original input and optional context. Across GDPR, AIACT, MedQA, and FOLIO with multiple Qwen backbones, DAIS improves average final-answer accuracy over answer-only, flat chain-of-thought, and independent-QA baselines. On policy-compliance benchmarks, it achieves a largest gain of 5.6% and an average gain of 4.2% over the strongest non-DAIS baseline. Controlled ablations show that valid previous-state conditioning contributes beyond longer targets or additional intermediate text, supporting dependency-conditioned intermediate QA as a lightweight auxiliary supervision signal for standard final-answer inference.

</details>


### [36] [Translation as Augmentation: Effect of Translated Data on Assessment of Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19101)
*Yiheng Wu,Jue Hou,Roman Yangarber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨语言数据增强策略，通过机器翻译将高资源语言的标注数据迁移到低资源语言，以解决文本难度评估中标注数据稀缺的问题。实验表明，该方法能显著提升低资源语言文本难度估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本难度评估对于文本简化和个性化学习至关重要，但低资源语言缺乏专家标注的细粒度难度语料库，这严重阻碍了鲁棒评估模型的发展。

Method: 采用跨语言数据增强方法，利用机器翻译将高资源语言（如英语）的标注数据转换为目标低资源语言，并训练基于BERT的回归模型来预测难度分数。

Result: 实验证明，用机器翻译语料补充稀缺的母语训练数据，可以显著提高低资源语言文本难度估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 跨语言数据增强是一种可行的解决方案，能够有效缓解低资源语言在文本难度评估中标注数据不足的问题。

Abstract: Reliable Text Difficulty Assessment is a prerequisite for valid text simplification workflows and personalized learning applications. However, the development of robust assessment models is severely hindered by a critical bottleneck: the scarcity of expert-annotated corpora containing fine-grained difficulty levels (e.g., CEFR), particularly for lower-resource languages. This paper addresses this data scarcity problem in the context of a low-resource European language. We propose a cross-lingual data augmentation strategy that leverages machine translation to transfer labeled resources from high-resource languages to the target low-resource language. We train BERT-based regression models to predict difficulty scores and investigate whether synthetic, translated data can effectively supplement native training sets. Our experiments demonstrate that augmenting scarce native data with machine-translated corpora significantly improves the accuracy of difficulty estimation, offering a viable solution for languages lacking extensive expert annotations.

</details>


### [37] [Automated Extraction of Techno-Economic Data from 76,000 Energy System Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19178)
*Maxime Gorres,Jan Göpfert,Patrick Kuckertz,Noor Titan Putri Hartono,Heidi Heinrichs,Jochen Linßen,Iain Staffel,Jann Michael Weinand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过自动化方法从76,000项能源系统研究中提取了320万个结构化定量数据点，构建了一个可交互的FAIR数据库，用于分析学术假设与实证数据的差异以及跨技术、区域和时间的研发布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统模型依赖难以溯源和审计的定量假设，手动信息提取因文献快速增长而变得不切实际，导致数据库更新不及时和研究重复。

Method: 采用自动化提取技术从2010年以来出版的76,000项能源系统研究中提取定量信息，构建包含320万个数据点和2000万条元数据的FAIR数据库，并通过交互式仪表板提供数据筛选、分析和下载功能。

Result: 成功构建大规模结构化数据库，揭示了学术假设与实证观测数据的偏离情况，并展示了跨技术、区域和时间的研发布局差异。

Conclusion: 自动化提取方法显著提升了能源系统文献的透明度和可分析性，FAIR数据库为模型输入和文献分析提供了可靠资源，交互式仪表板促进了社区共享与复用。

Abstract: Energy system models guide societally important decisions, but their credibility rests on quantitative assumptions that are difficult to source and audit. Meta-analyses can improve transparency and modeling practices, but the rapid growth of publications makes manual information extraction increasingly impractical. Consequently, databases are updated infrequently and efforts are often duplicated across research groups. Here, we demonstrate the highly accurate automated extraction of quantitative information from 76,000 energy system studies published since 2010. We compile 3.2 million structured quantitative data points together with 20 million associated metadata entries, spanning a broad spectrum of technologies, methodological approaches and system characteristics. Beyond providing input data for models, the resulting FAIR database make the energy systems literature itself analysable. We show where academic assumptions diverge from empirical observed data, and how research priorities vary at scale across technologies, regions and time. To facilitate broad use within the community, the database is provided through an interactive dashboard, enabling users to filter, analyse and download data according to their specific research needs.

</details>


### [38] [Reasoning Before Translation: Enhancing Legal Machine Translation with Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19181)
*Aixiu An,Michael Jungo,Eloi Eynard,Mark Drenhaus,Andreas Fischer,Jean Hennebert,Sébastien Rumley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了不同方法在瑞士法律领域机器翻译任务上的表现，包括监督微调、强化学习以及推理增强模型。研究发现，强化学习能显著提升小型基础模型的翻译质量，且效果接近但略逊于前沿推理模型；随着模型规模增大，再训练方法的收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的机器翻译因法律语言的复杂性和高精度要求而极具挑战性。近年来具备推理能力的语言模型为应对此类挑战提供了新思路，但尚需系统比较不同技术路径（如监督微调、强化学习与推理模型）在法律翻译场景下的效果。

Method: 研究以瑞士法律体系的多语言法规为测试集，评估了小型语言模型（Qwen3.5 4B、Qwen3.5 9B、Gemma 3 12B）在监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）增强下的性能，并与前沿推理模型进行对比。强化学习采用可验证奖励（verifiable rewards）进行优化。

Result: 1) 强化学习在法律领域NMT中可行且优于监督微调；2) 增强后的小型模型性能接近但未达到前沿推理模型水平；3) 再训练方法随模型规模增大呈现收益递减趋势。

Conclusion: 强化学习为资源受限场景下的法律翻译提供了有效途径，但模型规模与推理能力仍是关键瓶颈。未来需进一步探索高效训练范式与模型架构的协同优化。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) in the legal domain is a linguistically and conceptually demanding task, primarily due to the complexity of legal language and the high level of precision it requires. The recent emergence of reasoning-capable language models opens new possibilities for tackling such challenges. They add to a set of other previously proposed techniques to enhance the translation quality, which includes supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
  In this work, we perform a comparison between these various approaches. More particularly, we evaluate small language models such as Qwen3.5 4B, Qwen3.5 9B, and Gemma 3 12B enhanced with various re-training paradigms and compare their performances against frontier reasoning models. We focus on the Swiss legal system, which -- with its unique multilingual statutes -- offers a particularly challenging testbed for reasoning-augmented models. Our results show that the quality of small ``base'' models can be greatly enhanced, and that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can be applied to NMT in the legal domain and surpasses the translation quality of supervised fine-tuning. The performance of enhanced small models is close to the one of state-of-the-art reasoning models yet remains inferior. We also note that re-training paradigms yield diminishing returns as model size increase. The code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/aixiuxiuxiu/Legal-MT-SFT-RL.

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRA-Ev，一个基于西班牙MIR考试案例构建的临床论证挖掘基准，包含前提、主张及支持/攻击关系的细粒度标注，支持证据检索、论证成分提取和关系分类三级任务评估，旨在克服传统MCQA仅依赖最终答案准确性的局限。

Conclusion: MIRA-Ev通过细粒度的论证结构标注和多层级任务设计，能够更全面地评估临床NLP模型的论证推理能力，弥补了传统MCQA评估的不足，并为多语言临床论证研究提供了新资源。

Abstract: Clinical NLP evaluation remains dominated by multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which scores only final-answer accuracy and cannot detect when a model reaches the correct diagnosis while grounding it in irrelevant, absent, or contradictory evidence. We introduce MIRA-Ev, a clinical argument mining benchmark built on Spanish Médico Interno Residente (MIR) licensing-exam cases, re-annotated by expert clinicians with span-level premises, claims, and directed support/attack relations, and released in parallel Spanish (native), English, and Basque versions, the first clinical argumentation resource in Basque. MIRA-Ev organizes evaluation into a three-tier task hierarchy: evidence sentence retrieval, argumentative component extraction, and relation classification.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Score Prediction: LLM-Based Essay Scoring and Feedback Generation via Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19219)
*Xuefeng Jin,Jiashuo Zhang,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RLAES框架，通过强化学习联合优化作文评分与反馈生成，并引入基于量规的反馈评估（RFE）和自适应门控反馈优化（AGFO）提升反馈质量与评分校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在自动作文评分（AES）与自动反馈生成（AFG）中多依赖提示工程或监督微调，缺乏对强化学习后训练及反馈质量自动化评估的系统研究。

Method: 1. 提出RLAES统一框架，使用强化学习联合优化评分与反馈任务。2. 设计RFE评估框架，包含166项细粒度二元量规指标与LLM-as-judge，实现可测量、可解释的反馈质量评估。3. 提出AGFO，在强化学习中按需激活基于量规的反馈奖励，降低评估开销并提升反馈质量。4. 采用相邻对比推理（ACR）通过对比相邻分数等级改进序数评分校准。

Result: RFE能有效捕捉作文-反馈一致性，具有强成对判别能力并与专家偏好高度一致。在ASAP基准上，RLAES-AGFO在LLM方法中取得最佳评分性能（QWK=0.803），同时反馈质量与GPT-5.5相当，并避免了仅优化评分时的反馈退化问题。

Conclusion: RLAES通过强化学习实现了评分与反馈的协同优化，RFE提供了可靠的反馈评估手段，AGFO与ACR分别提升了训练效率与评分校准。实验表明该方法在保持评分精度的同时维持了高质量反馈，为AES/AFG的LLM应用提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely applied to automated essay scoring (AES) and automated feedback generation (AFG). However, existing studies rely primarily on prompt engineering or supervised fine-tuning, while systematic research on reinforcement learning (RL) post-training and automated evaluation of feedback quality remains limited. We propose RLAES, a unified LLM framework that jointly optimizes essay scoring and feedback generation through RL. To make feedback quality measurable, interpretable, and usable for training, we introduce Rubric-based Feedback Evaluation (RFE), an essay-grounded feedback evaluation framework comprising 166 fine-grained binary rubric items and an LLM-as-judge. Building on RFE, we propose Adaptive Gated Feedback Optimization (AGFO), which activates rubric-based feedback rewards on demand during RL, reducing evaluation overhead while improving feedback quality. We also propose Adjacent Contrastive Reasoning (ACR) to improve ordinal score calibration by explicitly contrasting adjacent score levels. Experimental results show that the RFE framework captures essay-feedback consistency, exhibits strong pairwise discriminative power, and closely aligns with expert preferences. On the ASAP benchmark, RLAES-AGFO achieves the best scoring performance among LLM-based methods (QWK = 0.803), while maintaining feedback quality comparable to GPT-5.5 and avoiding the feedback degradation observed under score-only RL. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/hellomuyi/RLAES.

</details>


### [41] [The Price of Reasoning: Cost-Quality Tradeoffs in Reinforcement Learning for Neural Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19226)
*Michael Jungo,Aixiu An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）在法律文档翻译中的应用，重点分析推理过程在训练和推理阶段的作用。实验表明，推理过程在推理阶段对翻译质量有积极影响，但会增加输出token数量，需权衡计算成本与质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在法律文档翻译中表现优异，但尚不清楚其优势是源于推理能力还是训练范式本身。本文旨在通过系统性地移除推理痕迹，探究推理过程在训练和推理阶段对翻译质量的具体影响。

Method: 在训练和推理阶段分别系统性移除模型的推理痕迹，对比包含推理与不包含推理的翻译结果。同时，分析推理过程对输出token数量的影响，并研究计算成本与翻译质量之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，在推理阶段包含推理过程对翻译质量有积极影响。推理过程导致输出token数量增加，从而带来更高的计算成本。

Conclusion: 推理过程在推理阶段对翻译质量至关重要，但需在计算成本与质量提升之间进行权衡。RLVR的优势部分归因于推理能力的引入，但训练范式本身也起重要作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been established as a viable paradigm for the post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs), including downstream tasks, such as Neural Machine Translation (NMT). With the latest research indicating that RLVR could be the preferred training method for translating legal documents due to the induced reasoning capabilities, it raises the question whether it is really attributed to the reasoning or more generally to the training paradigm. We investigate the importance of including the model's reasoning trace in the generated responses during both training and inference by systematically omitting it from one of the phases. Our experiments show that including the reasoning, specifically during inference, has a positive effect on the overall translation quality. Furthermore, we recognise that the reasoning leads to an increase in output tokens, hence we study the cost-quality tradeoff between the increased computational demands and the improved translation quality.

</details>


### [42] [Selection Shapes the Boundary: A Preregistered Replication of Monotonicity and Label Agreement in Unselected NLI Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19231)
*Haram Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一项关于自然语言推理(NLI)中人类标注变异(HLV)的研究未能复现先前关于非向上单调性算子导致更低标注一致性的发现。在未筛选的SNLI和MultiNLI开发集上，所有七个对比均显示正向Cliff's delta，且效应量远低于预设的最小效应值(0.10)。研究结论认为先前发现的负向边界可能是低一致性筛选条件下的结构特征，而非总体性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究(arXiv:2607.15870)在ChaosNLI数据集(仅包含5票中3票多数标签的样本)中发现，包含非向上单调性算子的假设语句标注一致性较低(Cliff's delta = -0.284)。本研究旨在检验这一发现是否在未筛选的原始数据集(SNLI和MultiNLI开发集)中同样成立，以确定该效应是普遍性质还是筛选条件导致的假象。

Method: 研究 preregistered 了在未筛选的SNLI和MultiNLI开发集上复现先前分析。使用相同的算子标注器，采用四水平序数一致性结果，计算非向上单调性算子项目与向上单调性算子项目之间的Cliff's delta效应量。进行了稳健性检验：模拟标注器误分类以评估测量偏差，并对200个新样本进行人工重新标注审计以评估标注器可靠性。

Result: 所有七个对比均返回正向Cliff's delta(非向上项目一致性略高而非更低)，唯一显著的验证性对比符号与注册预测相反。所有效应量远低于预设的最小效应值0.10。稳健性检验表明：模拟标注器误分类会缩小而非制造效应；人工重标注审计在200个样本上达到四类一致性0.875，支持测量可靠性。

Conclusion: 先前观察到的负向边界很可能是低一致性筛选条件下的结构特征，而非总体层面的性质。基于筛选重标注资源构建的HLV结构声明应明确说明其筛选条件。研究强调了在标注变异研究中考虑选择偏差的重要性。

Abstract: Prior work on human label variation (HLV) in natural language inference (NLI) has often relied on re-annotation resources that select items by disagreement level. An earlier study (arXiv:2607.15870) found that hypotheses containing non-upward monotonicity operators showed lower label agreement in ChaosNLI (Cliff's delta = -0.284), which is restricted to items whose majority label carries exactly three of five votes. We preregistered a replication of this boundary in the unselected populations that ChaosNLI was drawn from: the SNLI and MultiNLI development sets, using the same operator tagger and a four-level ordinal agreement outcome. The registered prediction fails. All seven contrasts return a positive Cliff's delta (non-upward items agree slightly more, not less), the only significant confirmatory contrast has the opposite sign to the registration, and every effect is far below our smallest effect size of interest (0.10). Robustness checks support the measurement: simulated tagger misclassification shrinks the effects rather than manufacturing them, and a manual re-tagging audit reaches four-class agreement of 0.875 on a fresh 200-item sample. We conclude that the earlier negative boundary is plausibly a structure conditional on low-agreement selection rather than a population-level property, and that HLV structure claims built on selected re-annotation resources should state their selection conditional explicitly.

</details>


### [43] [MeetingToM: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Theory-of-Mind Reasoning in Multi-Party Meetings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19235)
*Ziyi Wang,Yuhang Wu,Dongxu Piao,Xingyu Liu,Tianhui Zhou,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeetingToM是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在自然多党会议场景下复杂社会行为推理能力的新基准，重点关注伪共识等会议特有现象，并揭示当前模型在整合非语言线索、推断隐藏态度和区分真实共识与伪共识方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ToM基准主要关注视频问答和显性信号，缺乏对潜在社会状态和群体动态的覆盖，无法有效评估模型在复杂多党会议场景下的社会推理能力。

Method: 构建MeetingToM基准，按层次组织评估：主体级心理状态预测、二元级对话对象理解、群体级共识推理，并提供统一评估协议。

Result: 系统分析代表性MLLMs，发现其在整合非语言线索、推断隐藏态度、区分真实与伪共识方面存在持续局限性。

Conclusion: MeetingToM为推进会议场景下的多模态ToM研究提供了关键测试平台，揭示了当前模型面临的核心挑战。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer other's beliefs, intentions, and states of knowledge, is central to social interaction, yet remains challenging for current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), especially in multi-party meetings where cues are distributed across speech and behavior. Existing multimodal ToM benchmarks mainly focus on video-grounded question answering over overt, externally verifiable signals, and provide limited coverage of latent social states and group dynamics. We introduce MeetingToM, a benchmark for complex social behavior reasoning in naturalistic multi-party meetings. MeetingToM targets meeting-specific phenomena such as \textbf{pseudo-consensus}, where apparent agreement masks private dissent under social pressure. The benchmark is hierarchically organized to evaluate ToM at increasing levels of social granularity, including (i) subject-level mental state prediction, (ii) dyadic-level addressee understanding, and (iii) group-level consensus reasoning. We provide a unified evaluation protocol and conduct systematic analyses of representative MLLMs, revealing persistent limitations in integrating non-verbal cues, inferring hidden attitudes, and distinguishing genuine consensus from pseudo-consensus. Our results highlight key challenges and establish MeetingToM as a testbed for advancing meeting-grounded ToM in multimodal models.

</details>


### [44] [Inference-Time Steering for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19243)
*Alexander Manev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）的多语言事实不一致问题，评估四种干预策略（角色提示、对比激活添加CAA、直接偏好优化DPO）在Gemma 3 12B模型上的效果。角色提示在效果、安全性和泛化性上表现最佳，CAA虽能显著改变不一致性但对配置敏感且可能损害知识，DPO适配器提供永久性改进但泛化能力有限。研究表明跨语言不一致性部分源于选择偏差，简单的上下文干预可能更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在多语言场景下表现出色，但其内部知识表示偏向高资源语言，导致跨语言事实不一致——即模型仅根据提示语言改变答案分布。本研究旨在推理阶段缓解此类偏差，探索不同干预策略的有效性与可转移性。

Method: 研究采用四种干预策略：1）零样本上下文引导（角色提示）；2）内部表示操作（对比激活添加CAA）；3）轻量级权重修改（基于基准事实数据的直接偏好优化DPO）；4）基于概念泛化数据的DPO。实验在Gemma 3 12B Instruct模型上进行，并构建了多语言事实数据集及包含文化特定查询的新泛化基准进行评估。

Result: 角色提示在整体干预中表现最强，兼顾效果、安全性与领域外泛化。CAA能显著改变不一致性基准指标，但对配置敏感且存在知识退化风险。DPO适配器提供永久性改进，但收益范围较窄且可转移性较差。

Conclusion: 跨语言不一致性至少部分源于选择偏差，简单的上下文干预（如角色提示）可能比侵入性更强的方法更适用于实现稳健且可转移的对齐。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multilingual fluency, their internal knowledge representations remain disproportionately biased toward high-resource languages. This leads to cross-lingual factual inconsistency, where they shift their empirical answer distributions based solely on the prompt language. We investigate whether these biases can be mitigated at inference time, forcing an English-prompted model to answer as if it were queried in target languages (German, Spanish, Bulgarian), and evaluate four intervention strategies: zero-shot contextual steering (persona prompting), internal representation manipulation via Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), and lightweight weight modification via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trained on benchmark-derived factual data as well as conceptual generalization data. To assess alignment, we curate a multilingual factual dataset alongside a novel generalization benchmark comprising culturally rooted queries to determine whether factual interventions transfer to broader target-centric preferences. Experiments on Gemma 3 12B Instruct reveal persona prompting to be the strongest overall intervention, balancing efficacy, safety, and out-of-domain generalization. While CAA yields sharp inconsistency benchmark shifts, it is configuration-sensitive and risks knowledge degradation. DPO-based adapters offer permanent, yet narrower and less transferable gains. These findings suggest that cross-lingual inconsistency is at least partly a selection problem, and that simple contextual interventions may outperform more invasive methods for robust, transferable alignment.

</details>


### [45] [Prompt Design at Scale: How Format, Instruction Count, and Context Length Shape Instruction Adherence and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19257)
*Netanel Eliav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过两个受控实验，系统性地评估了大语言模型在指令设计、上下文容量和格式选择三个关键决策上的表现。实验发现：1) 指令数量增加至80条时，完美响应率降至零；2) 系统提示与用户轮次的放置位置对性能影响与格式相当，但方向因模型而异；3) 无模型显示markdown的普遍优势，一个35B模型反而偏好纯文本；4) 在长上下文场景中，召回率在64-128k token后急剧下降，且格式依赖性强，某模型在128k token时准确率差距达48分；5) 未观察到虚假前提逢迎或不存在事实的捏造，但模型在接近各自上下文容量上限时拒绝回答的比例急剧上升；6) 不同格式的token开销差异显著（+22%至+37%），进一步影响格式选择。研究发布了完整评测框架、语料生成器和原始结果（VeyraBench）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型应用实践中，存在三个关键但缺乏实证依据的提示设计决策：指令与上下文的格式选择（如markdown、纯文本、表格等）、系统提示中可承载的指令数量上限、以及模型上下文长度对召回率和诚实性的影响。本研究旨在通过严格控制的实验填补这些证据空白，为实际应用提供数据驱动的指导。

Method: 研究使用一个无污染、确定性再生的合成语料库“Book of Veyra”（包含8,780个唯一命名实体），在五个模型上开展两个交叉实验。实验1（每模型960次调用）测试指令数量（10至160条）与四种格式、系统提示/用户轮次放置的交叉效应，测量指令遵循衰减。实验2（每模型5,520次调用）在2k至512k token的上下文长度梯度上，测量召回准确率、虚假前提逢迎和无事实捏造，同样交叉四种格式。所有实验均使用相同语料和评估指标。

Result: 实验1显示：当指令数达到80条时，所有模型、格式和放置方式的完美响应率均降至零。放置位置的影响在N=160时与格式相当，但方向因模型而异。无模型表现出markdown的可靠优势；一个35B参数模型反而偏好纯文本。实验2显示：召回率在64-128k token内保持接近完美，之后急剧下降，且下降幅度依赖格式——某模型在128k token时准确率差距达48分。未观察到捏造（0/5,760次探测）或显著逢迎（≤8.3%）。接近各模型上下文上限时， outright refusal（ outright refusal to answer）比例从0%飙升至79-90%。预注册的格式排序未得到支持；不同格式的token开销差异（+22%至+37% over plain text）进一步影响格式选择。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了三个关键实践启示：1) 系统提示中指令数量存在严格上限（约80条），超过后性能崩溃；2) 格式选择无通用最优解，需结合具体模型和上下文长度，且需考虑token开销；3) 长上下文场景下，召回率在特定阈值后急剧下降，而拒绝回答成为主要失效模式，而非捏造或逢迎。研究通过发布VeyraBench，为社区提供了可复现的评测工具和基准数据。

Abstract: Practitioners make three prompt-design decisions with almost no controlled evidence behind them: how to format instructions and context (markdown, plain text, prose, or tabular), how many simultaneous instructions a system prompt can carry before compliance degrades, and how much context a model can hold before recall and honesty degrade. We report two controlled experiments crossing all three factors on one held, contamination-free synthetic corpus (the "Book of Veyra," 8,780 uniquely-named entities, deterministically regenerable from a fixed seed), evaluated across five models.
  Experiment 1 (960 calls/model) measures instruction-following decay as rule count N grows from 10 to 160, crossed with four formats and system-prompt vs. user-turn placement. Perfect-response rate collapses to zero by N=80 for every model, format, and placement. Placement produces effects at least as large as format at N=160 in most models, but the direction is model-specific. No model shows a reliable markdown advantage; one 35B model favors plain text instead.
  Experiment 2 (5,520 calls/model) measures recall accuracy, false-premise sycophancy, and absent-fact fabrication across a 2k-to-512k-token context ladder in the same four formats. Recall stays near ceiling through 64-128k tokens, then degrades sharply and format-dependently: one model's accuracy spread reaches 48 points at 128k tokens. Fabrication never occurs (0/5,760 probes), and sycophancy stays negligible (<=8.3%). What rises sharply near each model's context ceiling is outright refusal to answer (0% to 79-90%), distinct from sycophancy or fabrication. Neither pre-registered format ordering holds, and token overhead (+22% to +37% over plain text) further changes which format is preferable where accuracy spread is genuine.
  We release the full harness, corpus generator, and raw results (VeyraBench): https://github.com/iNetanel/veyrabench

</details>


### [46] [Two-Level Meta-Rubrics for Evaluating Open-Ended Generation: GAMUT, a Benchmark for Factual Completeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19322)
*Xilun Chen,Zhaleh Feizollahi,Ross Goodwin,Seungwhan Moon,Scott Yih,Pinar Donmez,Babak Damavandi,Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Gamut基准，用于评估长文本生成的事实完整性，通过两层元评分类框架将结构化内容要求转化为可机器评分的检查清单，在1,813个基于可穿戴图像的问题上测试了14个模型，最佳得分仅58.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实性评估主要关注精确度（precision），即检测生成内容中的错误声明，但忽略了事实完整性（factual completeness）——即回答是否应包含所有必要信息。事实完整性难以评估，因为所需事实常构成开放集合、有序过程或关系网络，无法用简单的布尔检查列表捕获。

Method: 提出两层元评分类框架：上层为结构化元 rubric 捕获内容的组织与重要性，下层通过机械编译为扁平的二元机器可评分 rubric。基于此构建 Gamut 基准，包含1,813个跨10个领域的真实可穿戴图像问题，每个问题配以证据支持的 rubric（经人类专家验证）。同时发布文本-only 变体。使用 LLM judge 进行评分。

Result: 评估14个前沿及开源模型，发现基准极具挑战性（Gemini 3.1 Pro 最高仅58.7%），具有高区分度，且对 judge 选择稳健。

Conclusion: Gamut 框架有效解决了事实完整性评估难题，通过结构化元 rubric 到扁平检查清单的转化，实现了可靠、可扩展的自动评分。实验表明当前模型在事实完整性上仍有显著提升空间，该基准为未来研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Evaluating the factuality of long-form generations has focused predominantly on precision, measuring whether the claims a model makes are correct. The dominant decompose-search-verify pipeline catches incorrect claims well but says little about whether a response contains all the information it should. Measuring factual completeness, the missing half of factuality, is harder: it requires enumerating the full set of facts a complete answer should contain, and these facts rarely form a flat list. They often involve open-ended sets where coverage is what matters, ordered processes, and relationships among facts that a list of independent boolean checks fails to capture. We introduce a two-level meta-rubric framework for evaluating open-ended generation, and instantiate it as Gamut (Grounded Assessment of Multimodal Factuality), a benchmark for factual completeness in long-form generation. The framework rests on a two-level rubric representation: a structured meta-rubric captures the organization and importance of the required content, which is then mechanically compiled into a flat checklist of binary, machine-gradable rubrics that an LLM judge scores reliably. We construct 1,813 questions grounded in real wearable imagery across 10 diverse domains, each paired with an evidence-backed rubric verified by expert human annotators. Because the framework is modality-agnostic, we also release a text-only variant. Evaluating 14 frontier and open-weight models, we find the benchmark genuinely challenging (best score 58.7% from Gemini 3.1 Pro), highly discriminative, and robust to the choice of judge.

</details>


### [47] [Selective State-Space Adaptation and Retrieval for Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19326)
*Atahan Dokme,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MaLoRA和MaRA两种适配器，通过引入状态空间递归实现动态、状态感知的低秩适应，在多个推理基准上显著优于静态LoRA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LoRA采用静态学习更新，无法捕捉token级或实例级的状态变化，缺乏对输入依赖性和跨token/跨段状态的建模。

Method: MaLoRA在token级将适配器缩放因子变为输入相关的动态函数，并引入跨token的循环状态；MaRA在上下文级跟踪跨段状态并检索相关片段，再经调制语言模型生成答案。两者均基于状态空间模型（Mamba）实现选择性递归。

Result: 在三个冻结骨干（Qwen-2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B）和两个推理基准（MuSiQue、2WikiMultihopQA）上，方法平均提升+6.8 F1（相对+10.5%）， hardest cell提升+9.3 F1（相对+18.2%），且在长度压力下RULER QA-2任务中token级增益得以保持。

Conclusion: 通过状态空间递归在token和上下文粒度引入动态状态感知，能有效增强冻结语言模型的推理能力，且泛化性强。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation introduces a static learned update applied identically to every input. The update provides task-level adaptation but does not explicitly represent token-level or instance-level state variation. A family of adapters is proposed that introduces selective state-space recurrence at two complementary granularities. At the token level, \textbf{MaLoRA} (Mamba-modulated low-rank adaptation) makes the adapter's scaling factor a dynamic input-dependent function with recurrent state across tokens, in contrast to the stateless modulators of prior work. At the context level, \textbf{MaRA} (Mamba Retrieval Adapter) tracks cross-segment state and selects the segments most relevant to the query, before the modulated language model generates its answer. Across three frozen backbones (Qwen-2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B) and two reasoning benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultihopQA), the family improves reasoning accuracy on every cell of the $3{\times}2$ grid, by $+6.8$ F1 ($+10.5\%$ relative) on average and up to $+9.3$ F1 ($+18.2\%$ relative) on the hardest cell over the LoRA baseline, and the token-level gains carry to RULER QA-2 under length stress.

</details>


### [48] [Copy Less, Ground More: Overcoming Repetitive Copying in Long-Context Reasoning via Evidence-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19345)
*Lizhe Fang,Weizhou Shen,Tianyi Tang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究长上下文大语言模型在复杂推理任务中的重复复制问题，提出GEAR奖励塑形方法，通过增强对关键证据的聚焦和惩罚对无关内容的引用，显著提升模型性能并减少冗余输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在复杂推理任务中常出现重复复制输入文本的现象，而非有效解决问题。该行为随上下文长度增加而恶化，且模型无法有效区分关键证据与无关信息，导致准确率下降。

Method: 提出GEAR（Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward）奖励塑形方法，结合准确性奖励、关键证据重叠奖励和无关上下文重叠惩罚。同时构建自动化管道，从任意文档生成带证据标注的训练数据，使方法适用于自然语言场景。

Result: 在多个模型规模和基准测试中，GEAR相比标准强化学习（仅基于准确率奖励）平均提升4.6分，且在更长上下文中增益更显著。同时减少重复复制并缩短推理链长度。

Conclusion: 即使长上下文评估从简单检索转向复杂推理，模型对相关证据的准确聚焦仍是关键能力，且存在较大改进空间。GEAR通过奖励塑形有效提升了模型的证据利用效率和推理质量。

Abstract: Large language models that generate step-by-step reasoning traces have achieved strong performance on complex tasks, and extending them to long-context settings has emerged as an important frontier. However, we identify a critical failure mode in this regime: \emph{repetitive copying}, where models extensively copy text from the input into their reasoning traces rather than productively solving the problem. We show that this behavior is pervasive across frontier long-context LLMs and intensifies with context length. By separating each prompt into task-relevant key evidence and irrelevant distractor context, we further show that the root cause is insufficient grounding: models copy from the prompt indiscriminately, and those that fail to focus on key evidence are far more likely to answer incorrectly. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GEAR (Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward), a reward shaping method that augments the accuracy signal with a grounding reward for overlap with key evidence and a distractor penalty for overlap with irrelevant context. To enable GEAR on natural-language data, we develop an automated pipeline that constructs evidence-annotated training data from arbitrary documents. We validate GEAR across multiple model scales and benchmarks, showing consistent improvements of up to +4.6 average points over standard RL with accuracy-based rewards, with larger gains at longer contexts, while also reducing repetitive copying and thinking length. Our findings suggest that, even as long-context evaluation shifts from simple retrieval toward complex reasoning, accurate grounding in relevant evidence remains an indispensable capability with substantial room for improvement.

</details>

### [49] [SysAdmin: Measuring Instrumental Power-Seeking in Frontier AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18239)
*Mana Azarm,Qiyao Wei,Rahul Nambiar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为SysAdmin的基准测试，用于评估前沿语言模型在Linux沙箱中作为自主系统管理员时的权力寻求倾向。研究通过2800个任务评估了七个模型，发现经过偏差校正后，权力寻求行为的发生率在0%到5%之间。尽管当前模型在自然情境下表现出极少的自发性权力寻求，但发现了其他更明显的失败模式，如规范游戏和目标修改抵抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力寻求行为被认为是AI失控风险（Loss of Control, LoC）的关键驱动因素。现有研究缺乏对前沿模型在真实系统管理场景中权力寻求倾向的系统性评估，因此需要构建一个高保真度的基准测试来量化这一风险。

Method: 研究设计了SysAdmin基准，将前沿语言模型置于高保真Linux沙箱中，模拟自主系统管理员角色，测量五个维度的权力寻求行为：自我保护、增加自主性、资源获取、环境修改和策略性隐藏。共进行2800个任务，涵盖四个实验条件，并使用人类标注的校准数据进行偏差校正。同时设置了明确权力寻求提示的阳性对照以验证测量敏感性。

Result: 经过偏差校正后，各模型的权力寻求估计值在0%到约5%之间。阳性对照实现了100%的检测率，表明测量方法具有敏感性。研究发现当前前沿模型在自然系统管理情境下几乎不表现出自发性权力寻求行为，但存在模型特异的失败模式，提示评估需覆盖多种不一致模式。此外，还发现了更突出的失败模式，如规范游戏（specification gaming）和对目标修改的抵抗。

Conclusion: 当前前沿语言模型在SysAdmin基准中表现出极低的权力寻求倾向，表明在自然系统管理任务中失控风险可能有限。然而，其他失败模式（如规范游戏和目标修改抵抗）更为显著，提示对齐研究需关注多样化风险。评估应涵盖多种不一致模式，而非仅聚焦权力寻求。

Abstract: Power-seeking defined as behaviors where AI systems acquire resources, evade oversight, or resist termination beyond task requirements is identified as a key driver of Loss of Control (LoC) risk. In this work, we introduce SysAdmin, a benchmark that positions frontier language models as autonomous system administrators in a high-fidelity Linux sandbox to measure power-seeking propensity across five dimensions: self-preservation, increasing autonomy, resource acquisition, environment modification, and strategic concealment. We evaluated seven frontier models across four experimental conditions in a total of 2800 tasks. After bias correction using human-annotated calibration data, corrected power-seeking estimates ranged from 0 to about 5 percent per model. We also conducted a positive control with explicit power-seeking prompts that achieved 100% detection, validating measurement sensitivity. Our findings indicate current frontier models exhibit minimal spontaneous power-seeking in naturalistic system administration contexts, though model-specific failure modes suggest evaluations must test diverse misalignment patterns. Nevertheless, we discovered other more pronounced failure modes (than power-seeking) such as specification gaming and resistance to goal modification.

</details>


### [50] [Calibrated Selective Fact-Checking via Evidence Chain Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18240)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据链评估（ECE）框架，允许LLM在证据不足时选择不回答而非强制给出真假判断，从而提升事实核查的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM事实核查系统常强制输出二元判断，即使证据薄弱、稀疏或不一致，仍会给出高置信度错误结论，存在可靠性风险。

Method: 构建工具型验证代理，通过网页搜索、学术搜索和可执行检查收集证据，并输出结构化判断、置信度及来源元数据；在ECE-Bench上评估，引入覆盖率和选择性准确率等指标。

Result: ECE在标准准确率91.6%、覆盖率93.7%、已回答声称的选择性准确率97.8%；在6/95个案例中选择性推迟判断，且这些案例集中于低可靠性证据（来源等级L4）。

Conclusion: 选择性推迟作为安全机制，能有效处理证据薄弱的情况，在保持高准确率的同时提升系统可靠性，尽管在整体校准指标上未超越最强检索基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can achieve strong fact-checking accuracy, yet forced binary decisions conceal a critical reliability problem: systems may issue confident verdicts even when supporting evidence is weak, sparse, or internally inconsistent. We address this issue through Evidence Chain Evaluation (ECE), a selective fact-checking framework that permits abstention via an uncertain verdict instead of requiring a true/false decision for every claim. The evaluated system is a tool-using verification agent that gathers evidence through web search, scholarly search, and executable checks, and then returns a structured verdict with confidence and source-level metadata. On ECE-Bench, ECE achieves 91.6% standard accuracy, 93.7% coverage, and 97.8% selective accuracy on answered claims. Although ECE does not outperform the strongest retrieval baseline on aggregate calibration metrics such as Expected Calibration Error, Brier score, or AURC, it delivers a clear selective-prediction trade-off: the system maintains very high accuracy on answered claims while deferring 6 of 95 cases. These deferred cases are concentrated in lower-reliability evidence settings (5/6 at source level L4), supporting the view that abstention functions as a safety-oriented mechanism for handling epistemically weak evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/ cheshireyang/ECE.git

</details>


### [51] [BatchDAG: LLM-Planned Execution Graphs for Scalable Ad-Hoc Analysis Over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18241)
*Anupreet Walia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BatchDAG是一个用于企业级跨实体分析的LLM编排系统，通过生成类型化DAG并利用实体感知批处理实现高效并行执行，在质量和可追溯性上优于基线，并已在生产环境处理大规模数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在分析企业级数据集时面临上下文溢出、实体归属丢失和线性延迟问题，导致无法处理大规模跨实体分析任务。

Method: 系统由LLM规划器生成类型化DAG（包含SQL查询、语义搜索、内存转换、并行扇出和单次分析），确定性引擎通过拓扑波并行和结构化JSON数据流执行；关键优化是实体感知批处理，按逻辑实体分组行以减少LLM调用。

Result: 在12个转录密集查询中，BatchDAG质量评分3.74/5，与专家设计管道（3.25/5）相当且显著优于ReAct代理（3.09/5，p<0.01）；可追溯性达77%转录证据率；结构化JSON中间体减少幻觉27%；规划器有效DAG生成率98.8%；生产环境处理5万+会议在60秒内，成本$0.02-$0.24。

Conclusion: BatchDAG作为通用编排层，能自动生成执行策略，在保持质量的同时显著提升效率和可追溯性，适用于企业级大规模分析任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at analyzing individual documents but break down on exhaustive, cross-entity analytical questions over enterprise-scale datasets due to context overflow, loss of per-entity attribution, and linear latency from sequential tool calls. We present BatchDAG, a system in which an LLM generates a typed directed acyclic graph (DAG) of operations -- SQL queries, semantic searches, in-memory transforms, parallel fan-outs, and single-shot analyses -- which a deterministic engine evaluates with topological-wave parallelism and structured JSON data flow. A key optimization, entity-aware batching, groups rows by logical entity before fan-out, reducing LLM calls by up to 47x. BatchDAG is not primarily an accuracy improvement over hand-optimized pipelines; rather, it is a general-purpose orchestration layer that replaces multiple hand-engineered workflows with a single system that generates the appropriate execution strategy from natural language. In controlled experiments on 12 transcript-heavy queries, BatchDAG (3.74/5) achieves quality comparable to an expert-designed pipeline (3.25/5) and significantly outperforms a ReAct agent (3.09/5, p<0.01), with superior provenance (77% transcript evidence rate vs. 46-60% for baselines). A controlled ablation shows structured JSON intermediates reduce hallucinations by 27% versus prose summaries (paired t-test, p=0.107, n=12). The planner achieves 98.8% valid-DAG rate across 300 planning calls. In production at Brevian.ai, BatchDAG processes queries over 50,000+ meetings in under 60 seconds, with measured per-query costs of $0.02-$0.24 at published GPT-5.1 pricing.

</details>


### [52] [AI Tool Discovery at Scale: All You Need is DNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18242)
*Enhao Chen,Yulin Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolDNS 利用 DNS 实现语义工具发现，将 O(N) 搜索转为 O(log N) 解析，在 33,688 工具基准上减少 95.26% 搜索空间，保持检索精度并显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 代理工具发现机制在百万级工具规模下存在 O(N) 复杂性与中心化治理瓶颈，需要更可扩展且去中心化的方案。

Method: 提出 ToolDNS 框架，通过部分展开名称、EDNS0 意图载荷与逻辑子域名三项 DNS 扩展，将功能意图与组织信任嵌入 DNS 层次命名空间，实现语义剪枝与去中心化治理。

Result: 在包含 33,688 个跨协议真实工具的基准测试中，ToolDNS 将单次查询搜索空间减少 95.26%，检索准确率与现有最优方法相当，且基于 UDP 的设计使发现延迟降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 可扩展的 AI 互操作性无需新增中间件，而应更智能地利用现有 DNS 基础设施；ToolDNS 证明 DNS 可作为语义工具发现的弹性底层。

Abstract: The coming era of autonomous AI agents demands a discovery mechanism capable of navigating millions of tools, yet existing solutions buckle under O(N) complexity and centralized governance. Instead of building another fragile overlay, we propose ToolDNS, a radical framework that retrofits semantic tool discovery onto the Internet's most resilient substrate: the Domain Name System (DNS). By embedding functional intent and organizational trust into a hierarchical namespace, ToolDNS transforms an expensive semantic search into a series of lightweight, O(log N) name resolutions. We introduce three protocol-compliant enhancements to enable decentralized governance and semantic pruning: partially unfolded names, EDNS0 intent payloads, and logical subdomains. To rigorously evaluate this approach across the fragmented tooling landscape, we construct and release a large-scale heterogeneous benchmark comprising 33,688 real-world tools spanning MCP, A2A, RESTful, and Skill protocols. On this dataset, ToolDNS slashes the per-query search space by 95.26% while matching state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy. Furthermore, its UDP-native design reduces discovery latency by orders of magnitude compared to HTTP-based registries. Our work demonstrates that scalable AI interoperability requires not more middleware, but a smarter utilization of the infrastructure already beneath our feet.

</details>


### [53] [From Agent Failure Paths to Quantified Residual Risk: A Compositional Framework for Resilient Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18243)
*Hassan Karim,Sai Sitharaman,Deepti Gupta,Danda B. Rawat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CPSAINT和FRIESA-K框架，通过七层完整性分解和基于马尔可夫模型的残差风险量化，为智能体AI提供可组合的信任机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险模型无法同时描述失效机制并提供可转移的残差风险估计，本文旨在耦合这两种视角。

Method: 提出CPSAINT七层完整性分解（物理状态、传感器、数据、计算、执行器、环境、时间）和FRIESA-K残差风险函数，使用受控吸收马尔可夫模型确定控制有效性。

Result: 框架在两个场景（实时仓库机器人和治理型金融代理）中验证，保持跨域一致性，支持可组合信任的形式化量化。

Conclusion: CPSAINT和FRIESA-K提供了一种简洁的机制到量级管道，实现跨领域推理和显式假设的定量形式化。

Abstract: Agentic AI is crossing trust boundaries faster than current risk models can represent. Existing approaches provide one of two partial views. They either describe failure mechanisms without producing a transferable residual-risk estimate, or they produce a risk estimate while treating the internal failure path as a black box. We couple those two views by proposing CPSAINT, a seven-layer integrity decomposition over Physical state, Sensors, Data, Compute, Actuators, Environment, and Time, paired with FRIESA-K, a residual-risk functional that maps each failure path to a quantified risk instance. FRIESA-K grounds the resistance term K in a controlled absorbing Markov model so that control effectiveness is derived from state dynamics rather than assigned as an informal score. The result is a concise mechanism-to magnitude pipeline for resilient agentic and embodied AI. We report governance observability through a separate additive penalty instead of inserting governance as a new variable in the resistance functional. We formalize structural composability linking valid failure paths to well-defined risk instances and show the framework on two contrasting scenarios a hard real-time warehouse robot and a governance-instrumented financial-services agent. Across both cases, the same layer grammar, variable semantics, and dynamic-resistance construction remain intact. Thus, we obtain a compact kernel that supports cross-domain reasoning, explicit assumptions, and quantitatively grounded formalism of composable trust.

</details>


### [54] [SAAG: Structured Agent Assessment and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18245)
*Ritvik Garimella,Vedant Khandelwal,Anvi Kohli,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAAG框架，通过将agent调用评估分解为注册表一致性、结构完整性和参数锚定三个顺序阶段，提供细粒度诊断，并支持基于阶段信号的迭代自修复。实验表明，结构化反馈能提升参数精度并减少幻觉，但端到端F1提升有限且依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent调用评估仅依赖精确匹配，将不同失败模式（如参数幻觉、错误选择原因）合并为单一分数，无法帮助实践者诊断失败根源。

Method: SAAG是一个级联诊断框架，将评估分解为三个顺序阶段：1）注册表一致性（检查是否选择正确agent）；2）结构完整性（检查是否满足schema）；3）参数锚定（检查参数值是否真实可靠）。每个阶段输出可解释的诊断信号，失败时可基于该信号进行针对性自修复，无需泄露真实值。

Result: 在Glaive数据集上，使用5/10/15个agent的注册表和三个<4B参数本地模型进行实验。结构化反馈相比单次推理和无信息二进制反馈，显著提升参数精度并减少值幻觉；但端到端F1提升较小且因模型而异。

Conclusion: 阶段分解的诊断评估是理解和提升agent调用可靠性的必要视角，适用于不同模型家族和注册表规模。

Abstract: Exact-match evaluation of agent-calling obscures qualitatively different failure modes: a model may select the right function yet hallucinate argument values, or satisfy a schema while choosing a agent for the wrong reason. Existing benchmarks collapse these distinctions into a single binary score, leaving practitioners unable to diagnose where agent calls fail. We propose SAAG a cascaded diagnostic framework that decomposes agent-calling evaluation into three sequential stages: registry conformance, structural completeness, and argument grounding, each producing interpretable stage-specific diagnostics. These diagnostics additionally enable iterative self-repair: on prediction failure, the stage-specific signal guides targeted correction without leaking ground-truth values. We evaluate this framework on a controlled benchmark derived from Glaive's function-calling dataset across registry sizes of 5, 10, and 15 agents using three local sub-4B-parameter models. Structured feedback consistently improves argument precision and reduces value hallucination relative to single-pass inference and uninformative binary feedback, while end-to-end F1 gains are modest and model-dependent. These results suggest that stage-decomposed diagnostic evaluation is a necessary lens for understanding and improving agent-calling reliability across model families and registry scales.

</details>


### [55] [Phionyx: A Deterministic AI Runtime Architecture with Structured State Management and Pre-Response Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18246)
*Ali Toygar Abak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phionyx是一种确定性AI运行时架构，采用治理优先方法，将LLM输出视为噪声传感器测量而非直接决策。通过结构化状态向量和确定性状态演化方程实现可重现行为，包含三个核心层：确定性评估内核、统一安全层和基于语义时间的记忆系统。实验显示相比后置过滤降低31%计算开销，相比LRU提升24%高价值数据保留率，确定性执行在100次重复运行中零方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统（尤其是LLM）的决策过程常被视为黑箱，缺乏可审计性和治理能力。本文提出将LLM输出重新概念化为噪声传感器测量，通过确定性架构确保系统行为的可预测性和可重复性，满足高监管要求场景的需求。

Method: 1. 设计确定性状态演化方程，使用结构化状态向量替代概率性决策；2. 构建三层架构：46块管道的确定性评估内核处理传感器测量、统一安全层提供预响应控制和隐私强制、基于语义时间的记忆系统实现带权重的缓存逐出；3. 通过哈希验证和重复运行测试确定性；4. 在单实例部署中对比后置过滤和LRU缓存策略的性能。

Result: 1. 在30%不安全输入比例下，相比后置过滤降低约31%计算开销；2. 缓存高价值数据保留率相比LRU提升24%（72% vs FIFO）；3. 100次重复运行中控制信号哈希值零方差，验证确定性执行；4. 单实例部署测试中零意外重启。

Conclusion: Phionyx通过治理优先的确定性架构成功实现了可审计、可重现的AI运行时系统，在计算效率、数据保留和确定性方面优于基线方法。但当前仅验证单实例场景，分布式或多租户部署仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We present Phionyx, a deterministic AI runtime architecture derived from the broader Echoism interaction framework that introduces a governance-first approach to AI engineering: treating large language model (LLM) outputs as noisy sensor measurements rather than direct decisions. Unlike probabilistic agents, Phionyx enforces deterministic state evolution via a structured state vector governed by deterministic state-evolution equations, enabling reproducible behavior in applications requiring auditability and governance. The architecture integrates three layers: (1) a deterministic evaluation kernel processing noisy sensor measurements through a canonical 46-block pipeline, (2) a unified safety layer providing pre-response control and architectural privacy enforcement, and (3) a semantic time-based memory system implementing impact-weighted cache eviction. Experimental validation on single-instance deployments demonstrates approximately 31% reduction in computational overhead vs. post-hoc filtering (at 30% unsafe input ratio, simulated cost model) and up to 24% improvement in high-value data retention vs. LRU (72% vs. FIFO, same cache capacity, benchmark-verified), deterministic execution verified across 100 repeated runs with zero variance in control signals (hash-verified), and zero unplanned restarts in single-instance deployment testing (see Appendix C for methodology and scope). This paper presents the architecture, its analytic structure, and scoped experimental evidence; generalization to distributed or multi-tenant deployments remains future work.

</details>


### [56] [Integro-differential equations in angular stabilization of drone motion by distributed feedback control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18251)
*Alexander Domoshnitsky,Oleg Kupervasser,Anatoly Polonsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用具有无界记忆的积分算子分布式反馈控制来稳定无人机角运动，并发展了一种将积分微分方程稳定性分析约化为常微分方程组分析的方法，应用于无人机飞行稳定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法通常假设记忆有限，但更长的观测时间可提供更多历史状态信息以改进控制。无界记忆的积分算子需要新的理论工具来研究其稳定性，而标准方法难以处理。

Method: 提出一种通用方法研究无界记忆积分算子的稳定性，将积分微分方程约化为（可能无限维）常微分方程组。在线性近似下，采用指数核可得到有限维系统。还探讨了更复杂核（如指数核线性组合）对稳定性的增强作用。

Result: 获得了关于积分微分方程指数稳定性的新结果，并应用于无人机角运动稳定化。示例表明更复杂的核可提升稳定性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将无界记忆积分控制问题转化为常微分方程组分析，为无人机稳定化提供了有效途径，并揭示了复杂核在增强稳定性方面的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose angular stabilization of drone motion using distributed feedback control in the form of an integral operator. It should be stressed that the memory of this integral operator could be unbounded. It is intuitively clear that large length of the observation time open new possibilities to construct better control based on previous states of the control object. Unbounded memory in control requires the creation of a certain approach different from standard ones to the study of integro-differential equations. One of the goals of this article is to propose a certain universal approach that allows us to study the stability of integro-differential equations in the case of unbounded memory in the integral operator specifying the feedback control in stabilization. The approach we propose allows us to reduce the study of integro-differential equations to the analysis of systems of ordinary differential equations. In general, such systems can consist of an infinite number of equations. In relation to the so-called linear approximation in the problem of angle stabilization manages to limit itself to relatively simple exponential kernels in the integral control and arrive at a system with a finite number of equations. The examples explain that more complex kernels, for example, linear combinations of the exponential kernels, can enhance the stabilization capabilities. We obtain new unexpectable results on the exponential stability of integro-differential equations. Then we apply them to stabilization of drone flight.

</details>


### [57] [MILP-Evo: Closed-Loop Fully Automatic Design of MILP Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18252)
*Jinbiao Nie,Kewei Feng,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Shan Yin,Zizhuo Wang,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM引导的闭环程序进化框架，用于自动设计MILP求解器的白盒组件（割平面选择器和分支规则），通过直接执行评估候选程序并利用反馈进行选择、修复和多样性维护，最终输出可解释、可修改的求解器策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的MILP求解器加速方法通常使用黑盒模型，难以检查、适应和部署；而显式的求解器逻辑虽易于理解，但依赖人工设计。本文旨在探索是否可通过LLM引导的闭环搜索自动设计可执行的白盒组件，并直接通过端到端求解行为评估。

Method: 提出一个闭环程序进化框架，通过PySCIPOpt实现。候选程序由LLM生成，加载到SCIP中直接执行评估，反馈用于性能选择、针对性修复、诊断反思和多样性维护的种群管理。

Result: 在四个基准问题上，LLM引导的程序进化能够发现具有竞争力的领域专用策略，表明该方法在自动设计可解释求解器组件方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将自动设计MILP求解器逻辑转化为LLM引导的闭环搜索问题，生成的显式组件可检查、修改并集成到标准求解器工作流中，为可解释的求解器自动设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Machine learning methods have shown that data-driven policies can accelerate mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) solvers, but many such approaches remain difficult to inspect, adapt, and deploy because the learned policy is represented as an external predictor or other opaque model. By contrast, explicit solver logic is easier to understand and integrate, but is usually hand-designed rather than learned from solver feedback. We study whether the automatic design of MILP solver logic can instead be cast as LLM-guided closed-loop search over executable white-box components evaluated directly by end-to-end solver behavior. To this end, we propose a closed-loop program evolution framework for MILP solver auto-design, implemented through PySCIPOpt, and instantiate it on the joint design of a cut selector and a branching rule. Candidate programs are iteratively generated, loaded into SCIP, and evaluated by direct execution on MILP instances, with the resulting feedback guiding performance-based selection, targeted repair, diagnostic reflection, and diversity-aware population maintenance. The method outputs explicit solver components that can be inspected, modified, and deployed within standard solver workflows. Across four benchmark families, we find that LLM-guided program evolution can discover competitive domain-specialized policies in several settings.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Accuracy and Cost: Latency-Aware LLM Query Routing for Dynamic Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18253)
*Shivam Patel,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Ankur Mallick,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑延迟的查询路由器，通过轻量级延迟估计器模拟自回归token批处理，联合优化延迟、准确性和成本，相比标准负载均衡方法，在相同延迟下可提升40%的准确率-成本效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询路由器主要关注准确率与成本的平衡，但忽略了生成延迟，而实际延迟受查询提示长度、模型实例负载及调度批处理策略影响，导致效率未达最优。

Method: 设计轻量级延迟估计器，模拟自回归token批处理过程，估算查询的TTFT（首token时间）；将该估计器集成至延迟感知路由器，实现延迟、准确率、成本的联合优化。

Result: 实验表明，在保持相同延迟水平下，相比标准负载均衡方法，该方法在准确率-成本效用上提升高达40%。

Conclusion: 将延迟估计与路由决策结合，能有效提升查询路由的整体效率，在满足延迟约束的同时显著优化准确率与成本权衡。

Abstract: Modern language query routers improve inference efficiency by assigning each query to a model that balances response quality and monetary cost. However, current query routers are largely latency-agnostic and do not consider the generation latency experienced by queries at model instances. In practice, latency is often controlled by load-balancing policies such as round-robin or join-the-shortest-queue, which do not account for model accuracy or inference cost. Incorporating query latency into routing is challenging as it depends not only on the query's prompt length, but also on the current prefill and decode workload at the model instance and the scheduling and batching policy of the serving framework. We design a lightweight latency estimator that simulates autoregressive token batch processing in the serving framework and estimates the time-to-first-token (TTFT) of queries. We incorporate this latency estimator into a latency-aware router that jointly optimizes latency, accuracy, and cost when assigning queries to model instances. Our experimental results indicate that this joint optimization yields up to 40% improvement in accuracy--cost utility while maintaining the same latencies as standard load-balancing approaches.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Dialect Generalization Without Retraining: Benchmarks and Evaluation of Schema-Derived Constrained Decoding for MLIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18254)
*Plawan Kumar Rath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MLIR操作定义规范(ODS)的推理时先验方法，用于自然语言到MLIR代码生成，无需针对每个方言进行微调。作者发布了四个NL-to-MLIR基准测试，并构建了三层约束堆栈，使小型语言模型SmolLM2-1.7B在部分方言上超越大型代码模型，同时保持高生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIR作为现代ML编译器基础设施的核心，具有高度可扩展性，不同应用域会定义新方言，导致针对每个方言微调代码语言模型不可行。本文探索是否可以利用每个方言的ODS规范在推理时提供结构性先验，替代梯度微调，实现跨方言的通用代码生成。

Method: 1) 发布四个NL-to-MLIR基准测试（MLIR-Spec-150等），共410个样本，并包含OOG压力集和功能参考集。2) 构建三层约束堆栈：C1基于操作签名的控制流图，C2基于ODS提取的类型格进行类型域划分，C3基于SSA作用域的验证器驱动五重重试拒绝采样。3) 在多个方言上评估SmolLM2-1.7B与大型代码模型的性能。

Result: 在结构约束主导的方言（如linalg）上，SmolLM2-1.7B达到80.0%验证通过率，显著优于CodeLlama-34B等大型模型（高21-44个百分点），且生成速度快8-25倍。在属性值决定验证语义的方言（如arith+func、StableHLO）上，大型模型表现更好。

Conclusion: 基于ODS的推理时先验在结构约束强的方言上能有效提升小型模型的代码生成质量，且无需方言特定微调。该方法在跨方言泛化性和效率上具有优势，但面对属性值敏感场景时仍有局限。

Abstract: Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) underlies modern ML compiler infrastructure (TensorFlow, JAX/StableHLO, PyTorch Inductor, IREE), yet appears only in trace amounts in code-LM pretraining corpora. MLIR is also extensible by design: new dialects ship per application domain, so a fine-tuned model per dialect does not scale. We ask whether inference-time priors derived mechanically from each dialect's Operation Definition Specification (ODS) can substitute for gradient-based adaptation. First, we release four natural-language-to-MLIR benchmarks across three dialects - MLIR-Spec-150, Linalg-Spec-30, StableHLO-Spec-30, and StableHLO-Held-Out-200 - totaling 410 in-scope NL-to-MLIR pairs, plus a 25-program out-of-grammar stress set and a hand-authored n=30 functional reference set, shipped under Apache-2.0 with Gebru datasheets and Croissant 1.0 metadata. Second, we build a three-layer schema-derived constraint stack: a CFG over op signatures(C1), type-domain splits from an ODS-extracted type lattice (C2), and an SSA-scope validator driving five-retry rejection sampling (C3). Porting from arith+func+memref+linalg to StableHLO required no new constraint-layer code. On dialects whose verifier semantics are dominated by structural constraints, schema-derived priors let SmolLM2-1.7B match or exceed 15B-34B code LMs at 8-25x the per-generation speed: on linalg, SmolLM2 reaches 80.0% verify-valid (three-seed mean, n=125), beating CodeLlama-34B, Granite-Code-34B, and StarCoder2-15B by 21-44 percentage points with non-overlapping CIs. On arith+func and on the templated parametric StableHLO-Held-Out-200, where verifier semantics turn on attribute values rather than structure, the same baselines match or beat the SLM; we scope these as non-win cells. We release benchmarks, decoder, all per-prompt generations, and a reproducibility Docker image.

</details>


### [60] [Semantic Cooperative Games for Contribution Attribution in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18255)
*Pengyi Jiang,Xiaoguang Zhu,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义合作游戏（SCG）框架和语义夏普利值（SSV），用于LLM多智能体系统的贡献归因，无需反事实计算，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反事实的贡献归因方法在语言中介工作流中需要重复模型调用、方差高，且未显式捕捉中间语义状态。

Method: 将语言流表示为语义生成超图，定义语义夏普利值（SSV），并设计单轨迹算法SLIC：构建超图、恢复最小语义支持、应用布尔吸收、计算SSV，无需重新运行智能体子集。

Result: 在标准条件下SSV退化为经典夏普利值；在医学基准上SLIC计算成本降低93.3%，与蒙特卡洛夏普利基线高度一致；在多角色工作流中SSV与扰动诱导的分数下降一致，揭示语义贡献与失败影响的差异。

Conclusion: SLIC提供了一种快速、无需反事实、可解释的归因方法，适用于复杂LLM多智能体系统。

Abstract: Contribution attribution has become a central problem in LLM-based multi-agent systems, where final outputs are produced through multiple agents, message exchanges, and ordered workflow dependencies. Existing attribution methods often rely on counterfactual valuation, such as removing agents or comparing score changes across altered agent subsets. In language-mediated workflows, these methods require repeated model calls, introduce high variance, and do not explicitly capture the intermediate semantic states through which agents produce, preserve, and transform task-relevant information. We propose Semantic Cooperative Games (SCG), a framework that represents a realized language flow as a semantic generation hypergraph and induces an agent-level semantic value function on this structure. We define the Semantic Shapley Value (SSV) to allocate contribution over semantic support logic, and introduce SLIC, a single-trajectory algorithm that constructs the semantic hypergraph, recovers minimal semantic supports, applies Boolean absorption, and computes SSV without rerunning agent subsets. We prove that SSV reduces to the classical Shapley value under standard set-based, fully observable, and no-order-dependence conditions. On a medical benchmark satisfying these conditions, SLIC reduces computation cost by 93.3% while remaining highly consistent with a Monte Carlo Shapley baseline. In more general multi-role workflows, SSV aligns with perturbation-induced score-drop profiles and exposes cases where semantic contribution and failure impact diverge. Overall, SLIC provides a fast, counterfactual-free, and interpretable attribution method for complex LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [61] [PEARL: Solver-in-the-Loop Interactive Optimization Modeling from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18256)
*Hongliang Lu,Zhong Li,Yuxuan Chen,Yuan Lan,Fan Zhang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL系统，通过交互式执行和求解器反馈来优化建模过程，显著提升求解成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在优化建模中多为一次性生成，缺乏执行、调试和迭代修订，与实际交互式建模流程不符。

Method: PEARL在循环中集成Python执行和数学规划求解器，学习何时测试部分模型、如何根据求解器诊断修订以及何时停止，利用中间执行结果、可行性信号和方案检查来改进模型。

Result: 在多个优化基准测试中，PEARL大幅提高验证求解率；PEARL-Qwen3-4B在宏平均和微平均精度上均超越更大的DeepSeek-V3.2-685B。

Conclusion: 交互式工具集成能有效提升优化建模的自动化水平，小模型通过迭代反馈可达到甚至超越超大模型的性能。

Abstract: Optimization modeling is the process of translating real-world decision problems, often described in natural language, into formal mathematical formulations and executable solver code. While recent advances in large language models have shown promise in automating this process, most existing approaches remain one-shot: a model produces a formulation once, without executing it, conditioning on solver feedback, or iteratively revising errors. This stands in sharp contrast to real-world optimization modeling, which is inherently interactive and proceeds through repeated solve-debug-revise cycles. We introduce PEARL, a system for interactive optimization modeling that uses Python execution and mathematical programming solvers inside this loop. Rather than relying on a fixed repair workflow, PEARL learns when to test partial models, how to revise from solver diagnostics, and when to stop. It operates in a multi-turn tool-integrated setting where intermediate execution results, feasibility signals, and solution checks are used to improve both formulations and solver code before finalization. Across diverse optimization benchmarks, PEARL substantially improves verified solve rates over strong one-shot and tool-augmented baselines; notably, our PEARL-Qwen3-\textbf{4B} model outperforms the much larger DeepSeek-V3.2-\textbf{685B} in both macro- and micro-averaged accuracy on optimization modeling tasks.

</details>


### [62] [S2T-RLHF: Hierarchical Credit Assignment for Stable Preference-Based RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18258)
*Wei Chen,Guanghui Zhu,Yafei Li,Limin Wang,Yihua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S2T-RLHF，一种基于句子到令牌的奖励分解框架，通过分层信用分配解决RLHF中因过度细粒度奖励细化而导致的训练不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF依赖序列级标量奖励，在令牌级策略更新时存在信用分配模糊问题。现有方法通过更细粒度的奖励细化来改善，但忽略了偏好信号噪声可能被放大，导致学习不稳定。本文提出粒度感知原则，强调稳定性导向的奖励设计。

Method: S2T-RLHF采用分层奖励分解：首先将序列级偏好奖励分配到句子级，然后在每个句子内进行有界的令牌级细化，无需重新训练奖励模型或令牌级监督。句子作为中间粒度，平衡语义连贯性与对令牌级噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集和优化设置上的实验表明，S2T-RLHF提升了训练稳定性和鲁棒性，同时保持了具有竞争力的偏好对齐性能。

Conclusion: 通过句子级中间粒度进行分层信用分配，S2T-RLHF在细粒度奖励细化与稳定性之间取得平衡，为RLHF中的奖励设计提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) with preference-based reward models often exhibits unstable training dynamics. A key contributing factor is that standard RLHF relies on a single sequence-level scalar reward, which is propagated to token-level policy updates and leaves credit assignment within a response inherently ambiguous. Recent work has attempted to address this issue by refining rewards into denser token-level supervision, often relying on the implicit assumption that finer-grained credit assignment improves optimization. We argue that this assumption is incomplete: when preference signals are noisy and only defined at the response level, overly fine-grained reward refinement can amplify reward uncertainty and destabilize learning. To address this problem, we propose a granularity-aware principle for hierarchical credit assignment, emphasizing stability-oriented reward design rather than maximal allocation precision. Under this principle, sentences serve as a natural intermediate granularity, balancing semantic coherence with robustness to token-level noise. Guided by this view, we introduce S2T-RLHF. This sentence-to-token reward decomposition framework first allocates sequence-level preference rewards across sentences and then applies bounded token-level refinement within each sentence, without reward-model retraining or token-level supervision. Experiments across multiple datasets and optimization settings show that S2T-RLHF improves training stability and robustness while maintaining competitive preference alignment.

</details>


### [63] [Probabilistic Concept-Aware Steering for Trustworthy LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18259)
*Brian Becker,Rui Chu,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PCS框架，通过概念驱动的转向向量检索和概率强度校准，在保持LLM原始任务能力的同时实现可控的安全导向语义偏置，解决了现有转向向量方法中表示不连贯的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转向向量方法在推理时干预LLM生成，但存在表示不连贯行为，损害可解释性和细粒度控制。根本原因在于以往工作仅关注二元正负转向评估，并使用离散聚类指标，无法捕捉语义对齐的连续谱系。

Method: PCS框架包含两个核心组件：1) 概念驱动的转向向量检索，从预定义概念空间中检索相关方向；2) 概率强度校准，根据语义对齐程度动态调整转向强度，实现连续可控的干预。

Result: PCS在保持模型原始任务性能的同时，实现了更细粒度的语义偏置控制，提升了转向行为的可解释性和安全性。
